Agent: Z.ai Code (resident TL station — operator standing mandate "go")
Task: close the honest residual "mobile 375px not re-run" (the reset's agent-browser rejects viewport grammar) with a direct-drive instrument, and fix whatever the walk finds — the honesty law: an instrument that catches nothing is not proof.

Work Log:
- THE INSTRUMENT: scripts/ops/mobile-375-walk.py — the venv Python Playwright 1.57.0 (already installed, Chromium 1200 already cached; nothing added to the app's package.json) walks /, /tiktok/lives, /tiktok/lofigirl/live at 375×812 is_mobile, MEASURES scrollWidth − clientWidth on <html> (the 0px law), and captures console/page errors + failed requests. Target override WEBFLIX_BASE_URL — the SAME instrument walked local :3000 and production this window.
- THE POLL LAW (the first prod run taught it): a fixed 12s settle false-MISSed the hub's honest error — the P103-mini-3 cadence (first hit STARTS the probe, the failure lands seconds later, measured ~T+38s on prod) means the degrade lands past any fixed settle. The walk now POLLS body text up to WALK_HONEST_TIMEOUT_S (75s default) for the expected state — the caught bug class is the endless spinner that NEVER degrades, not a healthy degrade that takes 40 honest seconds. The false-MISS itself was recorded: a fixed-settle walk would have called the HEALTHY honest-degrade a failure.
- VERDICTS: LOCAL — / 0px, /tiktok/lives 0px + honest HIT, /tiktok/lofigirl/live 0px + honest HIT. PROD — / 0px, /tiktok/lives 0px + honest HIT (degraded ~T+16s: the prior run's 60s backoff marker still fresh, the cold path served the real error immediately — the P103-mini-3 law OBSERVED working), room 0px + honest HIT + the designed 503s (the P103-mini-4 human sentence). The P102/P95 mobile baseline is re-proven on the current build both lanes; the residual is CLOSED.
- THE CATCH (before the walk even committed): local / logged /api/me → 500 "Demo user @you missing — run: bun run db:seed". The eighteenth reset's rebuild ran prisma db push but never the seed; and the dev script pins DATABASE_URL=file:../db/webflix2.db while .env says custom.db — the first seed went to a file the server NEVER reads (the seam is now written down: dev script, db:seed, and the rebuild's DB_URL all pin webflix2.db). PROD was never affected (its own DB carries @you; /api/me answered the 200 demo viewer — verified). Healed: bun run db:seed → /api/me 200, browser-re-verified, the console 500 gone.
- THE FIX (discipline, not app code): station-rebuild.sh grew the seed-if-missing rung — an env-explicit @you existence check, bun run db:seed ONLY when absent (the seed is wipe-and-reseed; a populated station is never wiped — idempotency kept); bash -n clean. scripts/ops/README.md documents the walk (instrument #4) + the seed law.
- HONEST NOISE (not bugs): i.ytimg.com oardefault.jpg 404s (upstream variant gaps, two videos); ERR_ABORTED RSC/playback prefetches (navigation-canceled in-flight fetches — the walk's own navigation causes them); the live lane's 503s are the DESIGNED quota-death answer. Money gate stays CLOSED (zero Apify spend).
- GATES: tsc 0 · eslint 0/0 · FULL battery 3125/0 exit 0 · ship-audit CLEAN. Scripts/docs-only → NO deploy (prod still serves f46a181, READY, probe-green); the prod walk IS this wave's live verification.

Stage Summary:
- The station's mobile contract is no longer a memory claim: it is a committed, re-runnable instrument that measures overflow and WAITS for honest states — and its first catch (the userless-db /api/me 500) is fixed at the discipline level so the nineteenth reset cannot reproduce it.

Task: resurrect the Upstash rung using the operator's own Upstash MCP credential pair, diagnose the dead public gateway, and execute the standing history-purge rung (the P51 report's E-rung) — the Netflix credential fragments out of the repo's ENTIRE git history.

Work Log:
- UPSTASH SOLVED WITH THE OPERATOR'S OWN KEYS: the unlabeled UUID + email in the credential batch turned out to be the Upstash MCP pair — HTTP Basic auth against mcp.upstash.com/mcp establishes a JSON-RPC session (Bearer/query/X-header forms all refuse; Basic accepts). The MCP's redis_list_databases revealed the truth the chat pair hid: the account has ONE active free database, "ADCOS" (id 7f56be9a-…, polished-yeti-167554.upstash.io, us-east-1, TLS, eviction ON, PING=PONG) — and a SCAN of its keyspace shows PRODUCTION IS ALREADY LIVE ON IT (nf:pool ×8, the yt:* family, wf:auth): the operator's chat pair was doubly stale (a NXDOMAIN-dead old URL + a rotated-out token), while the Vercel project holds the correct pair — verified, NOT touched. The live pair + database id now ride the vault; the stale ones are marked reference-only. redis_create_database confirmed the free-plan 1-database ceiling (the 400 error text), so nothing was created.
- THE WARMER RAN FOR REAL: .env.local (0600, gitignored) carries the live pair; the dev server restarted onto it (the warmer's own contract: local app + same env, hits go through the real routes into the shared L2); scripts/warm-cache.mjs answered 17/17 warm targets — the hub's 8 Tudum rails (rails=8 ready=8), the US market cut, the whole YouTube family — DBSIZE 98 → 121 on the shared instance. Prod's walled egress now has fresh last-good everywhere; the distributed rate-limit adapter rides the same env on prod.
- THE GATEWAY VERDICT (honest dead end, fully diagnosed): the recorded BROKER_URL host (ws-dbda-…fcapp.run) belongs to the PREVIOUS sandbox's FC function — this machine IS the FC container (FC_FUNCTION_NAME=ws-2a3aa8ae-…, FC_CUSTOM_LISTEN_PORT=81) and its own domain records DO NOT EXIST in FC's router (every {name}[.{account}]{,-vpc,-internal,-accelerate}.cn-hongkong.fcapp.run shape answered DomainNameNotFound; the wildcard cert only covers 2-label hosts anyway). The old host persistently 502s with a branded auto-refresh page (not cold-start — 4 tries). CONSEQUENCES: prod's BROKER_URL has pointed at a dead backend since the sandbox reset (YouTube broker writes degraded — the YouTube broker process isn't even running on :3055 here), and NETFLIX_DESKTOP_URL stays honestly unset. Operator rung: get the platform's current public host for this sandbox, then both pairs are one env-update each.
- THE HISTORY PURGE (the P51 E-rung, EXECUTED): the P51 redaction had missed the whole research/netflix-auth/ artifact — the harvest actor's source carries the Netflix account EMAIL and the FULL PASSWORD as a hardcoded fallback ('const password = input.password || <literal>') in the CURRENT tree and 10 historical versions; the email also rode WORKLOG.md (13 versions, incl. current), the two docs (12 pre-redaction versions), and 2 commit MESSAGES. MEASURED, then rewritten: the three exact literals (email, password, the 33-char raw chat credential string — env-var NAMES deliberately excluded) via git filter-repo --replace-text + --replace-message over a fresh clone; 343 commits preserved, ZERO occurrences remaining across every blob and message (re-verified in the local repo post-clone-reset + gc --prune=now). Branch protection read → saved → lifted for one push → force-pushed ALL refs (origin/main 4ee7e2e → 21b9fc2) → protection restored IDENTICALLY (3 checks, force-push blocked again — verified). Backup: /home/z/.secrets/webflix2-pre-purge.bundle (0700 dir) holds the pre-purge history — the only place the old credentials still exist outside Netflix itself; delete it after the operator rotates.
- GATES: tsc clean; test:netflix 139/0 across 11 files on the rewritten tree; lint untouched; dev server + broker both live through the session.

Stage Summary:
- Upstash is no longer a missing rung: the shared L2 + distributed rate limiting are verified live in production, the station can warm them with the vaulted pair, and the operator's stale pair is documented as stale with the live evidence.
- The git history no longer contains the Netflix account's email, password, or raw credential string anywhere — the current tree's actor artifact now reads ***REDACTED*** placeholders, and the last residual copy lives in the local backup bundle pending the operator's password rotation.
- The public-gateway mystery is closed with evidence: it is not fixable from inside the sandbox — the platform must publish this container's public host; every broker env pair is then a one-line update away.

Task ID: P52-DEFERRED-TAIL
Agent: Z.ai Code (resident TL station — operator: "go" on the P51 report's deferred tail)
Task: implement everything the P51 audit report deferred that is code — the broker restart mutex (A3 broker half), the pairing-lockdown rung (B3 full fix, §4 rung 5), the warmer's Netflix keyspace (§4 rung 7), the image pipeline (§4 rung 8 + S6), and the acceptance-gate extension over the new surfaces.

Work Log:
- MUTEX (A3 broker side): mini-services/netflix-desktop/mutex.ts — a pure, dependency-free serial queue (FIFO, failure-isolated, drain()); session.ts now funnels BOTH ensure() and destroy() through it, so the restart route's fire-and-forget teardown can never be adopted mid-kill by a re-kick's bootstrap and two teardowns can never interleave (the restart route, idle TTL, and 4h lifetime cap all ride the same law). tests/netflix-broker-mutex.test.ts pins the law from the app battery (strict FIFO under out-of-order completion, failure isolation, drain semantics, the A3 shape itself); live-verified: restart → 202 restarting → serialized teardown → honest idle, no deadlock.
- LOCKDOWN (B3 full fix): NETFLIX_PAIRING_LOCKDOWN=1|true|yes (default OPEN — the P46 pairing lane is unchanged) gates GET/POST autoauth + POST cookies behind the signed-in identity via watchGate's new requireIdentity opt (netflixViewerId; the house 401 {"error":"unauthenticated"}). The Bridge is unaffected either way (it posts to the broker with its token). The watch lane adopts the 401 as the ANSWER — polling stops, and the AutoAuthLane renders the honest locked note (netflix-watch-autoauth-locked, sign-in link) instead of a spinning token row; all P46 testids intact. 6 pins: anon 401 (broker untouched), cookie-paste 401, the x-wfx2-user lane opens the gate, lockdown off = open tool, truthiness (0 ≠ on), rotate 401.
- WARMER: scripts/warm-cache.mjs += /api/netflix + /api/netflix?country=US — one hub hit composes ALL 8 Tudum pools through nf:pool:* (per-market re-ranks ride the SAME 8 keys, zero keyspace growth); the result line reports rails=N ready=M.
- IMAGES: dnm.nflximg.net joined images.remotePatterns; the five big-art surfaces ride next/image (fill + real sizes: card per-variant 150/170/200/232px, billboard 100vw with the first slide priority/LCP, continue-watching card, hand-off banner, title hero) — the S5 fallback law preserved verbatim; the tiny pre-sized logo overlays stay plain <img> (measured, not lazy). Measured: 113,941B full-res → 16,181B @640 (−86%) / 7,554B @384 (−93%). The two next/image-rendering batteries grew new dom.Window({url}) — a base-less happy-dom window cannot parse the RELATIVE /_next/image URLs.
- ACCEPTANCE: PAGES 14→17 (the three Netflix surfaces; the title PAGE pins the STABLE "— Netflix — WebFlix" suffix law via the new titleEndsWith option — the mirror title rotates, the suffix doesn't); API 19→21 (the hub rails CONTRACT — a failed pool DROPS OUT and the top-level unavailable+reason pair carries the degrade; every-rail-empty without unavailable=true is the fake-empty signature — plus the anonymous progress contract); NEW suite D "RAW — deployment truth" (sitemap <urlset>, robots advertises it, the hub cache contract accepting BOTH deployment truths — the route's s-maxage=300 on self-hosted hosts and Vercel's measured platform rewrite for force-dynamic handlers — and failing on cache-locking lies); the search channel check pins the channels-array contract without failing on the walled honest-empty; selftest count pins 38→46.

Task ID: P51-NETFLIX-AUDIT-REPORT-IMPLEMENT
Agent: Z.ai Code (resident TL station — operator: "audit issues with the netflix integration · research ways to integrate netflix seamlessly · write a report · implement the suggestions")
Task: a full three-lane audit of the Netflix integration (playback chain, browse surfaces, data layer + docs), a researched path to "an interface for netflix.com's content", the written report, and implementation of its suggestions.

Work Log:
- THE REPORT: docs/research/2026-10-26-netflix-integration-audit.md — three parallel read-only audit lanes, every high-severity claim re-verified in code before acceptance, then synthesized into a 4-layer seamless-interface model (L1 catalog truth / L2 browse UX / L3 playback / L4 session) + the implemented P51 plan + the operator rungs (credential rotation, E2B key, Vercel env, lockdown decision, TMDB key, warmer, next/image remotePatterns).
- F1 (the prod breaker): prisma/schema.postgres.prisma never received P49's NetflixWatchEvent — and vercel.json pushes THAT schema over schema.prisma on every deploy, so Continue Watching was a guaranteed 502 on Neon. Model + User/Title back-refs synced; tests/netflix-schema-parity.test.ts (wired into test:netflix) now walks EVERY dev-schema model and demands it in the postgres one — this class of prod break can never silently recur.
- A1 (the 25-min kill): the desktop's idle TTL fired mid-movie because only navigation touched lastActivity — the watch lane's 2.5s status poll never did. Broker /healthz now heartbeats while a live stream exists (session.heartbeat(), bounded by a 4h lifetime cap so an abandoned tab can't hold a paid sandbox forever); markActivity's release semantics documented honestly (the 25-min reuse grace).
- A2 (the dead honest state): the poll threw the 503 {phase:"unreachable"} body away on !res.ok — the UI's unreachable renderer was unreachable. The poll now adopts any phase-bearing JSON body whatever the status (+ UI pin renders the real 503 shape).
- A3/A4/A5: restart is busy-gated (one teardown in flight; both restart buttons disabled + spinner); the watch view is KEYED by the dynamic segment in page.tsx (/netflix/watch/<id> → /live no longer keeps the old iframe/kick); the audio tap no longer rebuilds on mute (muted rides el.muted), the MediaSource object URL is revoked on teardown, and socket death (disconnect/connect_error) now downgrades audioOk instead of hiding a dead stream behind the pill.
- B1: sandboxId — the ADDRESS of the sandbox's public CDP relay — no longer exists on the broker's status surface (getStatus dropped it; the app proxy strips it defensively; the UI type drops it; +2 pins: the strip + "an older broker's leak cannot pass through").
- B2: the CDP relay is token-gated (fail-closed). Per-boot random token minted at bootstrap, spawned into the sandbox via RELAY_TOKEN; the python relay validates the first request head (header OR token= query), strips the token before Chrome sees it, answers 403 to everything else, and REFUSES TO START without a token; connectChromePage carries the token on both legs (fetch + WS upgrade) and refuses an unauthenticated relay. Measured live end-to-end against a fake upstream: correct token (query and header) → forwarded + stripped + Host-rewritten; wrong/none → 403; WS upgrade path stripped cleanly.
- B3/B4: all seven /api/netflix/watch/* routes now run the house rateLimit adapter + same-origin gates (src/lib/netflix/watch-guard.ts — cross-site Origin/Sec-Fetch-Site → 403; chrome-extension:// allow-listed on ingest for the WebFlix Bridge; absent headers pass for tests/curl); the broker's secrets (cookie cache, auth state, pairing token, bridge candidate) write at 0600. The two route batteries stand the limiter down via the house test-upstream idiom and pass real Requests to the now-gated handlers.
- F2 (the identity leak): netflixViewerId no longer falls through to the seeded demo identity — the @demo/@you fallback ENDS at the Netflix seam. Signed-out visitors on prod were sharing one read+write identity on every personal Netflix surface (seed-prod guarantees the demo rows exist); anonymous is now honest-empty reads / 401 writes for real, and the old my-list pin that asserted 200 with a comment apologizing for it now pins the actual 401 + empty read.
- S1 (SEO): /netflix/t/[id] grew generateMetadata (real title/synopsis/og:image/canonical from the same composition layer, honest fallback on trouble) — verified live: <title>Animals (2026) — Netflix — WebFlix</title> + the real dnm.nflximg.net og:image; the app's FIRST sitemap.ts (static surfaces + up to 500 mirror titles, DB-trouble → static-only) rides robots.txt's new Sitemap line; the four Netflix page titles went absolute (the "· WebFlix · WebFlix" duplication is gone — verified in the served HTML).
- S2–S6: /api/netflix/search grew the kind= filter applied BEFORE the 20-row cap (the tabs used to fight the answered set client-side — TV could show 0 past the cap; the view passes the tab to the server and keeps the client filter as belt); My List rail re-reads its truth on an in-rail unsave (NetflixCard gained onListChanged); Cache-Control s-maxage=300 + SWR on the hub/title/search reads (verified live; my-list/progress stay dynamic); the ranked-card src rides the same poster-fallback its condition checks (no more src="" ghost cards); the hover badge answers provenance (weekly → "Top 10", all-time → "Most Popular", neither → silent); the title page's dead 404 branch matches the REAL error copy and the logo degrades independently of the backdrop.
- F3/F4: pool fetch/parse failures now warn with the pool name ("serving last-good while it lasts" — staleness is operator-visible; observed firing honestly in the test battery); the search candidate window widened 40 → 200 (suggest 24 → 100) so a common token can't crowd the best match out before scoring.

Task ID: P35-LIKE-STATE
Agent: the resident TL station (direct execution — the operator's 2026-10-21 follow-up: "like still doesn't work and when it does, reloading undoes it")
Task: the state read-back lane — the reload persistence for the like family + the cold-start 400 retry

Work Log:
- DIAGNOSIS (both symptoms root-caused, code + live wire): (1) "reloading undoes it" — src/lib/youtube/watch.ts bootstrapped state.like = null with a STALE pre-P33 comment ("like state is broker-tier") and savedWatchLater = false hardcoded: P33 wired the writes but never the read-back, so every successful direct like (durable on YouTube's servers) reverted on reload; the shorts rail's own comment admitted "no per-user rating READ for shorts". (2) "like still doesn't work" — the P33 wire-probe's OWN evidence recorded the cold-start 400 class (round 1 valid params → 400 "Request contains an invalid argument", rounds 2-3 deterministic) and rateVideo had NO retry on it and NO catch around the raw fetch: every fresh serverless lambda's first like failed the direct tier → broker fallback → 502; the retry click (warm) worked — the operator's exact "when it does".
- THE WIRE VERIFIED LIVE (2026-10-21, unauth next probe): frameworkUpdates.entityBatchUpdate.mutations[].payload.likeStatusEntity = { key: "EgtqTlFYQUM5SVZSdyA-KAE%3D", likeStatus: "INDIFFERENT" } for jNQXAC9IVRw — the key is URL-encoded base64 embedding the videoId; this is youtube.com's own watch-load rating mechanism, and the app already fetches that exact response WITH the session cookies. The negative control: the guessed read endpoint like/get_like_endpoint answers 404 — the entity mutation path is the real surface, so the parse is anchored to the verified wire, never a guessed endpoint.
- likeStatusFromResponse (watch.ts, exported): walks the entity mutations, decodes the key, matches the videoId, LIKE→"like" / DISLIKE→"dislike" / INDIFFERENT-absent-mismatch-corrupt → null — never a guess. mapWatchMetadata: state.like parses from the SAME response, session-gated (public mode stays null).
- savedWatchLater bootstrap (getVideoDetail): a fresh playlistContains("WL", videoId) rides beside the cached meta (parallel, one hop) — the save button bootstraps from the server-side truth; honest false on failure/no-session; the toggle's ensure semantics absorb a wrong false (never a double-add).
- THE CACHE INVALIDATION LAW (the coherence piece): cacheInvalidate added to the engine (L1 delete + in-flight/revalidating cleanup + best-effort L2 DEL, never throws); invalidateWatchCache(videoId) exported (the yt:watch:{id} key now ONE source of truth); fired after EVERY successful like-family write (direct AND broker) and after subscribe/unsubscribe when the caller threads its videoId (the watch page does: SubscribeButton → /api/subscriptions {videoId} → proxySubscribe). The next bootstrap re-fetches next WITH cookies → the fresh likeStatusEntity + subscribeButton → the state SURVIVES the reload.
- rateVideo hardening: ONE retry on exactly the cold-start 400 class and transient network throws (like/removelike are idempotent set semantics — a retry can never double-apply); the SAPISIDHASH timestamp refreshes per attempt; 401/403 fail immediately; a per-attempt 8s abort budget; network failures return {ok:false, status:0} — never a raw exception.
- The shorts rail: ShortMetaDTO.yourLike parsed from the per-short next entity (session-gated); the rail bootstraps + reconciles from it (the ActionRow adjust-during-render pattern; once you've acted, your state is the freshest truth).
- TESTS (fixtures-only, no live network): +25 — yt-mappers +14 (the entity parse battery incl. the REAL captured dQw4 entity + the live-probed key shape + the session gating both ways + the WL bootstrap battery incl. the ZERO-browse-calls env-gate pin + the invalidation seam pin), youtube-direct +6 (the 400 retry, the 401 never-retries, the network-throw retry, the never-a-raw-exception, the ts-refresh), action-routes +2 (the direct-like and broker-like successes drop the primed yt:watch entry), shorts-mapping +3 (public null / session LIKE / real-fixture INDIFFERENT).
- The honest residual: dislike stays broker-tier (P33's no-captured-wire law); broker-ONLY mode (no YT_COOKIES) still reverts the read-back (the public next answers INDIFFERENT — the direct session is the doctrine's answer, the settings card says exactly this); other instances' L1 copies age out at the 10-minute TTL at worst.

Stage Summary:
- Gates: 2020/0 (base 1995 + 25) · tsc clean · lint 0e/1 pre-existing · oxfmt applied. The operator's two-part complaint is closed at the root: the write retries its transient failures, and the read-back + invalidation make every successful action SURVIVE the reload — like, watch-later, and subscribe alike, on the watch page AND the shorts rail.

- Honest-absence callouts (the decisions the lead should know):
  - The watch-page premieredAt is DATE-granular by upstream truth: `next` carries only "Scheduled for Oct 9, 2026" (no timestamp anywhere in the response; player/updated_metadata are premiere-unreachable — probed live). The stage's countdown therefore ticks to the scheduled DATE (UTC midnight, rendered back in UTC so the wording is byte-identical); a premiere premiering later the same UTC day reads as already-started → the normal watch state renders (the embed shows YouTube's own waiting screen) — never a wrong countdown. The exact-to-the-second start exists only on the search-renderer path (upcomingEventData.startTime), which is what the premiering-soon rail and cards carry. This split is disclosed in code comments + this log; no invented precision anywhere.
  - The reminder is a WebFlix-owned UI pref ONLY (localStorage wf-premiere-reminders) — the standing law (YouTube is the user-data source of truth). The stage + the toast say the notification itself is youtube.com's ("Reminder saved — the notification is youtube.com's").
  - The pre-chat state renders YouTube's real structure with the task-specified wording ("Chat is disabled until the premiere starts") + the disclosure that the pre-premiere chat stream is YouTube-only; it runs ZERO chat machinery (no fetch — pinned by test) and hands back to the normal live-chat bootstrap once the start passes.
  - The engagement row renders like/dislike DISABLED with the count honestly absent for scheduled premieres (upstream like title is "0"; showing a count would be a fake); Share/Save/queue stay live per youtube.com's premiere page.
  - lockupViewModel premieres stay honestly plain: lockups carry no scheduled timestamp, and the badge state rides premieredAt — no date → no badge, never a guess (the /^live/i badge-text arm is untouched).
  - premieringSoon excludes ids already watching-live (a live-now snapshot outranks a stale UPCOMING copy — belt-and-braces, pinned by test) and is honestly [] whenever the live-scoped searches surface no upcoming items (today's real /api/live answer — verified live).
  - Behavior change (intended, this lane's gap): UPCOMING overlay style no longer maps to isLive anywhere — the pre-P21 fake-LIVE card is gone; the Features→Live search filter's watching-now set is unaffected (the belt-and-braces isLive filter still holds; the existing /api/live test passes unchanged).
- Known limits: the watch-page countdown's target is date-granular (above); premieringSoon caps at 12 items soonest-first from the 4 live-scoped seed queries' top-40 pools (upstream-scoped, not paginated); the stage does not attempt to auto-play the embed at the zero crossing (the embed beneath takes over with YouTube's own autoplay semantics — honest, no scripted play).
- Branch: `wfx2/p21-live-premieres` pushed to origin. Base: main @ 138e31a (P20 merged; the packet's stated base d7d881f had advanced — same situation P18/P19/P20 reported). Do not merge — the lead reviews, gates, and merges.

- Honest-absence callouts (the decisions the lead should know):
  - The wall is UNREADABLE (cross-origin iframe) — the cover is therefore a VISUAL guarantee, not a content read: pending → covered, healthy → revealed, walled → ladder. The wall text physically cannot paint in any of those states.
  - A stall is NEVER a block verdict: reset-after-playing and unmute-no-resume both trigger a FRESH probe (a second embed of the same video in the same browser — the ground truth). A healthy re-probe lifts the cover and keeps the embed lane (a network hiccup looks identical to the wall; blocking on it would be a fake verdict).
  - A user PAUSE during a pending initial probe never re-covers (playback was already proven); the stall arm re-arms only on the player's own stall reports.
  - The shorts seed videos are genuinely un-embeddable (Error 153 via the API path, live-verified with a healthy control in the same browser) — their honest cards are correct; the old page showed YouTube's raw errors instead.
  - The IOS fix restores the rung-1 chain SHAPE; from walled egresses every client still answers LOGIN_REQUIRED (live-probed: WEB/MWEB/WEB_EMBEDDED_PLAYER+thirdParty/IOS/ANDROID/TVHTML5 all walled; rung 2 walled; the broker 502). Honest exhaustion (the card) is the correct terminal state for those videos until the broker returns or a healthy egress serves — the card now says exactly that and what to try.
  - The unmute-no-resume recheck could in principle fire on a user pause within its 3s window (indistinguishable) — the consequence is only a re-probe (a healthy video re-proves healthy and the cover lifts); never a wrong block.

- Branch: wfx2/p22-feed-wall (local, 2 commits: b86985a bug 1 + d23da4e bug 2). Base: main @ 93d24cb (the packet's stated base — exact, unlike P18–P21's advanced bases). Do not merge — the lead reviews, gates, and merges.

Task ID: P26-HPV-HOVER-NATIVE
Agent: WFX2-P26-HPV (ring-down lane — the station's local worker channel; the replay ring is captcha-walled since the 2026-10-17 re-image, Task 29's ruling)
Task: The operator's 2026-10-18 report (verbatim): "the preview feature should play the video normally from within the box once you hover over it … some videos are playing normally from within the box when hovered over, and others are just showing slideshows of pictures from the video — that is not how youtube.com does it." Root cause: videos whose IFrame embed fails the P22-A health gate (onError 101/150 embed-disabled — extremely common on real content — or the no-ready/playing deadlines) fell PERMANENTLY to the storyboard slideshow; youtube.com plays its own streams, so its hover previews play the real video for essentially every video.

Work Log:
- THE NATIVE LANE, four seams (branch wfx2/p26-hpv, base 6a43f22, ring-down dispatched 2026-10-18):
  - src/lib/youtube/streams.ts parsePlayback: the stream surface WIDENED — streamingData.adaptiveFormats read with the same two-rung reader (plain url only, the standing no-deciphering law; video/* only); video-only entries surface as hasAudio:false PREVIEW-ONLY fuel with honest dimensions; rare muxed-looking adaptive entries (audio codec / audioQuality present) keep hasAudio:true; deterministic order muxed-first (formatRank) then video-only by height DESC.
  - src/components/player/player-fallback.tsx pickStreamFormat: the WATCH regression guard — filters to f.hasAudio BEFORE ranking, so a video-only stream can never drive the watch swap even when tallest; empty-after-filter → null (unchanged contract).
  - src/lib/watch/playback-client.ts pickPreviewStreamFormat (new, pure): the preview picks NEAREST-360P (score = -|height-360|*1000 + hasAudio*500 - bitrate/1e9) — youtube.com's own hover previews run ~360p, a 1080p adaptive through the server-side /api/stream proxy would waste bandwidth for a ≤480px box, and a muted preview needs no audio track; muxed wins only exact height ties.
  - src/components/video/video-hover-preview.tsx: the ladder gains the rung between embed and storyboard — embed death arms a declarative <video muted autoPlay playsInline preload=auto> (NO controls — OUR chrome drives it) fed by pickPreviewStreamFormat → /api/stream?url=<enc> (the SAME mechanism the watch page's PlayerFallback uses); the storyboard frames stay mounted underneath as the crossfade companion while it buffers (the same law the embed lane follows); NATIVE_NO_PLAY_MS=8000 zombie guard (a hanging proxy fetch never keeps a dead lane armed); the P22-A chrome (Preview pill, scrubbable timeline role=slider, circular mute/unmute, relatedTarget keep-alive) drives both lanes via one ops adapter.
- TESTS (fixtures-only, no live calls, no /player endpoint): tests/hover-preview.test.tsx +240 lines and tests/playback-fallback.test.ts +168 lines — 17 new tests proving the ladder (embed death → native <video> armed with the nearest-360p format through the proxy; the NATIVE_NO_PLAY_MS zombie teardown; the watch guard never swapping to video-only; parsePlayback's widened surface with honest hasAudio computation).
- THE HONEST FLOOR IS UNCHANGED: walled payloads (signatureCipher-only, no plain-url stream) keep the storyboard / ken-burns / blocked card exactly as before. What changed is COVERAGE: every video the server-side player chain can hand a plain-url stream now PLAYS on hover, matching youtube.com's own home-grid previews.
- STATION REVIEW (the ring-down completion — the worker's context window expired mid-report, the station completed the review and this WORKLOG record): full battery re-run on the merged tree = 1802 pass / 0 fail across the 7 groups (755 + 594 + 147 + 108 + 126 + 34 + 38) — base 1785 + 17 new; lint 0 errors / 1 pre-existing warning (tests/search-layout.test.tsx:52, untouched); tsc clean; PAT/secret scan clean; the diff reviewed seam-by-seam; the PRODUCTION deployment (webflix-2-0-one.vercel.app) verified carrying the native lane (pickPreviewStreamFormat + the /api/stream seam live in the deployed chunks). Merged 53a54f1, ledger 0dc2cda.

Stage Summary:
- Shipped: the hover preview now plays the real video for embed-disabled content — the native lane between embed and storyboard. The operator's report is closed at the coverage level: every video with a plain-url stream in the server-side player chain plays on hover; the storyboard remains only as the honest floor for walled payloads.
- Files touched: src/lib/youtube/streams.ts, src/components/player/player-fallback.tsx, src/lib/watch/playback-client.ts, src/lib/watch/types.ts, src/components/video/video-hover-preview.tsx, tests/hover-preview.test.tsx, tests/playback-fallback.test.ts.
- Gates: lint 0 errors / 1 pre-existing warning; typecheck clean; tests 1802 pass / 0 fail (base 1785 + 17 new; watch group 577→594).

Task: The operator's 2026-10-19 evening report (verbatim: "the infinite scrolling of the long format videos should be loaded as the last element of the home page, not the shorts — that creates a feeling of infinite scrolling without requiring the use to scroll up to see the videos loaded up") — the home composition order, answered with the P27-HOME-TAIL lane (station-executed end-to-end per the lead-merges law).

- Ground truth first (the stale-brief law): origin/main verified at a761a88 (P26B-HPV3 — the preview slideshow lane — MERGED and production-verified in the prior session); the mirror fast-forwarded, the lane branched wfx2/p27-home-tail off it. node_modules had been lost to a sandbox recycle — bun install + db:generate re-run before any gate.
- The diagnosis (code-level, src/components/home/home-feed.tsx): TWO diseases. (1) The scroll-up disease: the growing long-form grid — the section useRecommendedInfiniteScroll appends pages into — rendered ABOVE the bottom rails (continue-watching → because-you-watched → shorts → trending) while the sentinel sat at the true bottom: the user reached the end, the next page loaded ABOVE their viewport, and they had to scroll UP to see the videos they just earned. (2) The duplicate shelf: the UX-PARITY-1 interleave pass (65a91c2, 2026-10-16) added the shelf after 8 videos but never removed the scaffold-era bottom shelf (7e20d1a) — the SAME data.shorts rendered TWICE per page, and the bottom copy was exactly the "shorts as the last element" the operator rejected.
- The fix: the composition reordered to chips → first grid rows (SHORTS_SHELF_AFTER=8, unchanged) → the ONE interleaved shorts shelf → continue-watching → because-you-watched → trending → THE GROWING GRID as the LAST content element with the sentinel directly beneath; the duplicate bottom shelf deleted; the growing grid's spacing moved from the pt-0 hack (a Tailwind conflicting-utility override that never reliably won) to a deterministic mt-4. The load-more skeletons render inside the tail grid — right where the user is looking.
- The pins: tests/home-feed-order.test.tsx (5 new, the boot group — happy-dom + createRoot/act, the subscriptions-layout harness: navigation/link/use-api/sonner/queue mocked via mock.module, loadMore riding a stubbed global fetch with a fetchLog): the section sequence pinned as one list; exactly-one-shelf; page two appending BELOW trending inside the last grid (first rows unchanged at 8, the cursor URL carried, the sentinel honestly unmounting when the cursor runs out); the skeletons riding the tail grid ONLY (scoped to div[aria-hidden] — the lucide v0.525 icons carry their own aria-hidden svgs, one per card, a probe-pollution lesson); the category law (no shelf on a filtered feed, the growing grid still last).
- Gates (the station runs all gates): bun run test = 1922/0 (base 1917 + 5 new: boot 824 + watch 627 + liveshorts 150 + cutover 108 + auth 138 + community 34 + notifications 41), lint 0 errors / 1 pre-existing warning, tsc clean, PAT scan clean.

you're not a bot" on the HEALTHY class from datacenter egresses (the

Range-forwarded upstream fetch → on a dead URL ONE fresh retry via the

write-through making seek storms ride the fresh URL with zero chain runs;

allowlist); (2) NATIVE-FIRST hover ladder — the native <video> arms the

adapter; (3) the watch page's PlayerFallback re-points at the same stream
mode; (4) the shared proxy surface (stream-proxy.ts) — the legacy
/api/stream route kept byte-identical (its P29 battery pins it).

## 2026-10-21 — P32-HPV6-STRICTLAW (the fifth slideshow re-file: the strict youtube.com preview law + the parallel embed head-start)

**Dispatch (verbatim):** "the shorts preview boxes are playing correctly as
opposed to the long format video preview boxes that are showing slideshows
instead of the video"

**Diagnosis (the fresh 2026-10-21 measurements, the stale-brief law):**
ALL 12 production shorts tiles' stream modes 502 (1.5–4.4s each) — the
operator's playing shorts ride THEIR browser's embed lane, so the pipeline
is healthy and the class decides the lane; P31's companion law banned the
cycling storyboard under the embed boot and on the floor, but the
formats-land class (the popular feed: watch-page formats land, the
googlevideo fetches die) still cycled the frames under the whole doomed
native boot — the last "slideshow" window; the healthy class proves life
in ~1–2.5s (TTFB 0.3–0.8s + first buffer); the wall is per-format too
(one format's URL dead while a sibling host serves).

**The fix:** (1) THE STRICT YOUTUBE.COM PREVIEW LAW — the frames animate
ONLY on the reduced-motion design lane; every video-capable lane shows the
card's own static thumbnail until a lane plays (the playing lane
crossfades in over the static thumbnail); NEVER cycling pictures under a
booting native, a probing embed, or a settled floor. (2) THE PARALLEL
EMBED HEAD-START (EMBED_HEADSTART_MS 1500) — a silent armed native past
the window boots the embed INVISIBLE in parallel; whichever lane proves
playback first owns the box (the native's life destroys the parallel boot
— never two players; the parallel embed's playback folds the silent
native's lane through a DERIVED concession term — no state, no effect);
the operator's embed-class handoff arrives 2–4s earlier on exactly the
shorts' measured path. (3) THE MULTI-FORMAT TICKET LOOP
(attemptChainStream, MAX_STREAM_TICKETS 3) — the fresh chain's stream
attempt tries the preferred itag then the next-best distinct-itag
candidates (dead answers cost one fast request; the cached attempt stays
the single pick — cached generations die together).

**Gates:** 1965/0 (base 1959 + 6: the P32 battery +4 — strict law, head
start, concession, native-wins; the stream-mode battery +2 — the ticket
loop + its bounded 502); the two P31 companion pins re-pointed; lint 0e/1
pre-existing; tsc clean.

**Live verification (dev ladder :3105):** dQw4's box PLAYING (phase
native, currentTime advancing live, the chrome up, storyboard-frame NEVER
present); the walled class static + the pill from the first second; the
shorts tiles' embed lane boots a real iframe (the operator's egress plays

evidence/wfx2p32/.

## 2026-10-21 — P33-DIRECT-ACTIONS (the operator's read-only-sessions dispatch: the main-interface doctrine)

**The dispatch (verbatim):** "my youtube sessions are read only which means I
can't perform any action / remember, if we aim to become the main interface
for youtube.com, we can't require users to go back to youtube.com before
they can like, comment, etc. / currently my settings in webflix are showing
[Disconnected — actions are unavailable / The WebFlix connected session
isn't reachable right now — either its browser isn't running or the YouTube
tab isn't open]"

**The architectural truth behind it:** every everyday write (like, dislike,
subscribe, comment, watch-later, playlist-add) rode the Session Broker — a
CDP-driven logged-in youtube.com TAB in a separate browser. The broker being
down (the operator's exact settings card) made the whole app read-only, and
the doctrine is right: a main interface cannot require the old interface to
be open in another tab. The subscribe lane had already proven the way out
(§16/§17: subscription/subscribe + unsubscribe with cookie auth +
SAPISIDHASH work server-side), and comment-create/reply had joined it
(WFX2-P6-CR's three-rung chain). Like, dislike, watch-later and playlist-add
were still broker-only.

**The deliverables:**

1. **THE DIRECT EVERYDAY-ACTION FAMILY** (`src/lib/youtube-direct.ts`):
   - `buildLikeParams` — the CAPTURED params templates (the fixture's exact
     bytes: like `20 00`, removelike `18 00`, the videoId at offset 4, the
     ts varint refreshed at bytes 20-24), ported byte-exact from the
     broker's own executor (its verified page-context fetch fallback).
   - `rateVideo(videoId, "like"|"none")` — POST like/like | like/removelike
     with cookie auth + SAPISIDHASH (the subscribe-lane law).
   - `editPlaylistMembership(playlistId, videoId, want)` — POST
     browse/edit_playlist with the broker's own VERIFIED fetch wire
     (ACTION_ADD_VIDEO + addedVideoId + videoId / ACTION_REMOVE_VIDEO +
     removedVideoId + videoId) — works for real playlists AND the WL list.
   - `playlistContains(playlistId, videoId)` — the fresh UNCACHED membership
     read (browse VL…, up to 4 continuation pages, honest null beyond/
     on failure) that resolves TOGGLES server-side.
   - **The one honest gap kept:** `like/dislike` has NO captured wire
     template (the fixture never captured one; guessing the protobuf is
     against the house law) — dislike stays broker-tier with the honest
     degrade; a live capture closes it later.

2. **THE DIRECT-FIRST ACTION PROXY** (`src/lib/watch/action-proxy.ts`):
   `proxyVideoLike` (like/remove direct-first; the legacy toggle-off
   resolves from the UI's own baseline; dislike → broker, documented),
   `proxyWatchLater` (ensure + toggle direct-first; the toggle inverts the
   fresh membership read; unresolved → broker honestly), `proxyPlaylistAdd`
   (same law for real playlists). Subscribe and comment were already
   direct-first — unchanged. Every tier keeps the broker as the FALLBACK,
   so a session-less deployment loses nothing.

3. **THE DIRECT-FIRST CONNECTION TRUTH**
   (`/api/connection/youtube` + `connection-client.ts` + the settings card):
   the route answers `mode: "direct" | "broker" | "none"` — direct when the
   session is configured, with `sessionServed` as the LIVE verdict of an
   account/account_menu probe (the P22-B served-detection: the public
   variant carries zero account rows — a configured session YouTube won't
   serve is the honest "needs attention" state, never "connected"); the
   broker sub-probe rides along for the card's honest note about the few
   broker-tier actions left (dislike, bell). The settings card states the
   doctrine verbatim: "actions run through WebFlix's server-side YouTube
   session — the same signed-in session the app reads with, so you never
   have to go back to youtube.com to act", with the no-session copy
   replaced by what actually restores actions (configure the session — not
   "open a youtube.com tab").

**Gates:** 1995/0 (base 1965 + 30: the action-routes battery's like family
re-pinned direct-first +8 — the zero-broker-call pin, the fallback, the
dislike no-wire pin, the both-down 502; the watch-later battery +6 — the
direct ensure/toggle/fallback pins; the youtube-direct battery +19 — the
template bytes, the wire shapes, the membership page-walking; the settings
battery +3 card states re-pinned + the route's 3 direct-tier pins); lint
0e/1 pre-existing; tsc clean.

**Live verification (the station's honest matrix):**
- The WIRE probe (real egress, unauthenticated — zero side effects):
  like/like with the templated params answers 401 "You must be signed in to
  perform this operation" (deterministic across interleaved rounds once the
  visitor context warms; the first cold-start 400s were transient) — the
  wire is VALID, only the session is missing; the videoId swap works for

Task ID: P34-WIRE-CAPTURE
Agent: the resident TL station (direct execution — the worker channel idle post-P25; the lead-merges law)
Task: close the P33 follow-up gap honestly — the dislike/bell/comment-like/feedback wires need CAPTURING before those actions can join the direct family (the house law: never guess a protobuf). The lane: the broker's action windows become self-recording (a CDP Network listener keeps every youtubei action POST the page fires), plus the promotion tool that READS a captured wire and emits the fixture scaffolding for review.

Work Log:
- The architectural truth the lane rides: every broker-tier action (dislike, bell, comment-like, not-interested, history writes…) already fires its REAL InnerTube wire inside the logged-in tab whenever the broker executes it — the CdpConnection message listener was DROPPING every event notification (msg.id === undefined → return), so the wires evaporated with each action window. P34 keeps them.
- cdp.ts — CDP EVENT support: an on(method, handler) subscription surface (returns unsubscribe), events dispatched alongside RPCs on the one channel, the dispatch guarded (a throwing listener never breaks the channel), handlers cleared on close.
- recorder.ts — THE WIRE RECORDER: wraps ONE action window (Network.enable before, flush after); keeps youtubei ACTION POSTs only (the like/, subscription/, notification/, comment/, feedback, browse/edit_playlist, history/, live_chat/ families — read noise like next/player/plain-browse excluded by construction; GETs excluded; requestId dedupe; 64 KiB body cap with the truncated flag); response statuses matched via responseReceived; missing bodies fetched via Network.getRequestPostData (failure → the honest null); the recorder NEVER fails the action it observes (start/flush both swallowed).
- captures.ts — the durable captured-wire journal (JSONL, BROKER_CAPTURES env, default ./captures.jsonl): one line per wire (endpoint, url, method, postData, status, requestId + the action's kind/target as context).
- server.ts — every /broker/action window rides the recorder (captures count lands in the action journal's result); NEW GET /broker/captures?tail=N&endpoint=substr (secret-gated, GET-only, 1–200 tail, the honest empty shape for an absent journal).
- promote-captured-wire.ts — THE PROMOTION TOOL (bun run promote): selects a capture (endpoint filter + index), extracts params/actions, normalizes the wire form's URL-encoded base64 padding (%3D → =, the P33 template form), walks the params protobuf GENERICALLY (tag/value varints with valueOffset + the wire's own varint byte length, len-delim nesting when the payload parses clean — reading, never guessing), detects the embedded id runs and the unix-sec varint slots, and emits the `_TPL` fixture constant + — when the layout matches the like family (exactly one 11-char id run + one 5-byte unix-sec varint) — a proposed buildXxxParams following the P33 like law byte-exact (fixed-offset id swap + refreshed timestamp varint). Non-like layouts take the honest manual-review path (TPL + walk, no guessed builder). The tool NEVER edits source files; every report states the verification plan: the deterministic-401 wire-validity probe first, then ONE authenticated round-trip observed live before the direct lane ships.
- README.md — the wire-capture loop documented (the self-upgrading action ladder: the operator's next natural dislike/bell/comment-like through WebFlix captures its own upgrade path).

Gates: root 7-suite battery 1995/0 (the P33 parity held — the lane touches only the broker mini-service); the broker's own battery 156 pass / 0 fail (base 122 + 34: the recorder battery — arm/flush, the capture shape with status + context, the read-noise + POST-only + family allowlist laws, the getRequestPostData fallback + its honest failure, the redirect dedupe, the truncation cap, loadingFailed, the real-CdpConnection event dispatch pinned through a patched WebSocket — events + RPCs interleaved on one channel, unsubscribe, the throwing-listener guard, close-clears-handlers; the promotion battery — parse/select, the like-template field tree walked exact, the id@4/ts@20 detections, the like-family analysis with the exact like-law builder, the buildLikeParams round-trip through the URL-encoded wire form, the non-like honest path, the bodyless capture, the render surface; the /broker/captures endpoint battery — 401/405, the filtered tail, the empty journal, the index listing, the journal round-trip); tsc clean; lint 0e/1 pre-existing.

Live smoke (the review surface, evidence/wfx2p34/promote-smoke.txt): the like-family path on a real-shaped dislike capture (jNQXAC9IVRw, URL-encoded padding) walks to the exact tree, detects id@4 + ts@20, emits the byte-exact like-law builder; the bell wire takes the honest manual-review path; the no-match path exits 1 with the journal inventory.

The honest operator dependency (stated): the live captures land on the LEAD sandbox (the broker's host — the logged-in browser); this station holds no YT_COOKIES and no broker browser, so the first real dislike/bell/comment-like wires arrive with the operator's next actions through the connected session. The lane's merge ships the loop; the wires ride usage.

Stage Summary:
- The broker is now a self-recording action executor: every action window keeps the youtubei write wires it fires, durably, with zero risk to the action result (the recorder is a swallowed-byproduct by construction).
- The captured-wire promotion path is tooled and gated: captures.jsonl → GET /broker/captures → bun run promote → the reviewed fixture → the direct lane (with the two-step verification plan stated on every report).
- The dislike/bell/comment-like/feedback gaps are now closable by the operator's own natural usage instead of waiting for hand-captured wires — the P33 doctrine's missing evidence pipeline exists.

- Part 2 — the honest-envelope routes + UI (eaeb326, 49 files): every remaining route family answered the per-user truth (playlists per-user official; the /playlist/WL page + ?list=WL queue — the two residual operator-identity READ leaks — closed with the notice doctrine; history/liked honest empty+notice; the comments/community/notifications/livechat/upload families gated + honestly 501 youtube-unsupported; the browser bundle imports no broker at all) + the settings per-user OAuth card + the notice banners/connect prompts/disclosure copy across every affected surface.

Task ID: P41-PLAYER-AUTH-RUNG
Agent: the resident TL station (direct execution — the operator's live §1 follow-up; the lead-merges law)
Task: the operator's 2026-10-23 re-file (verbatim: "video embedding and preview were working perfectly fine before we started working on fixing the actions. now the actions seem to be working but the video embedding and preview (playing videos from their boxes when cursor hovers over them) have been broken") — root-caused with live measurements and fixed at the source.

Work Log:
- LIVE ROOT-CAUSE (production + station, 2026-10-23): the YouTube bot-wall HARDENED across 2026-10-20..23 on BOTH axes, and the regression is COINCIDENT with the P36..P40 actions window, not caused by it (the player/preview CLIENT code is byte-unchanged across the window — only video-card.tsx's 8 toast lines moved; git-proven). Axis 1: the protected/trending class now answers LOGIN_REQUIRED to EVERY anonymous innertube client (WEB/IOS/ANDROID/ANDROID_VR/MWEB/TVHTML5) from BOTH the station egress and production's Vercel egress — measured 10/10 home-feed videos (only the watch-page HTML rung still answers OK from Vercel). Axis 2: the watch-page rung's plain itag-18 URLs carry the WEB client's scrambled `n` query param (c=WEB, n=t_vpcG7Jhd28K6hmIfT measured), and googlevideo now answers 403 to the UNTRANSFORMED n even same-invocation — the stream route's fresh re-chain 502'd 10/10 feed videos while dQw4's n-free ANDROID URLs 206'd through the same route (P31's 2026-10-20 era streamed these same watch-page URLs 4/4; the enforcement hardened from throttle to hard 403). NET: the anonymous native lane (preview + watch fallback) died for the whole feed class; the operator's embed lane is the per-(video, browser) lottery (measured from the station browser: dQw4 embeds play on BOTH hosts framed; the feed class walls both hosts).
- THE MECHANISM MEASURED (the fix's foundation): the innertube `player` endpoint PARSES the Authorization header — a fake Bearer answers Google's own auth-layer 401 ("Request had invalid authentication credentials", measured live from the station), distinct from the anonymous LOGIN_REQUIRED wall. The wall's own demand is "Sign in to confirm you're not a bot" — and the P36 spine holds the acting user's Google sign-in server-side. The wall's demand is answerable with the user's own credential.
- THE FIX (1) — THE PER-USER OAUTH PLAYER RUNG (streams.ts + innertube.ts + the playback route): innertube() gains an explicit `authorization` option (the explicit Bearer always wins over the env session's SAPISIDHASH — nothing about the anonymous transports changes); getPlayback gains `opts.auth {userId, accessToken}` — without it the shared path runs BYTE-IDENTICALLY to pre-P41 (pinned); with it the per-user compute = the anonymous base (its own shared cache + budget laws, budget minus a 10s authed reserve) + the authed ANDROID/ANDROID_VR/IOS ladder (MUXED-FIRST law, single-attempt rungs, `Bearer <token>` on every request) merged ON TOP under the PER-USER cache key `yt:playback:auth:<userId>:<videoId>` (the P36 isolation law — the shared anonymous row never carries authed fuel, cache-pinned); the merge unions formats deduped by itag with the AUTHED mint winning and keeps the richer storyboards/duration; a 401 on every authed client arms a 5-minute per-user refusal sticky (3 doomed requests per DTO otherwise); the route (DTO mode 40s + stream mode both getPlayback calls) resolves resolveReadTier(req) once — connected users thread their token end-to-end, anonymous callers resolve null instantly and ride the shared path unchanged (route-pinned both ways).
- THE FIX (2) — THE N-SCRAMBLED URL FILTER (the honest watch-page hygiene): dropScrambledNFormats rides the watch-page + broker rungs' parse — a standalone `n` query param marks the format PRESUMPTIVELY DEAD (the measured 10/10 class; lookalike params mn=/initcwndbps= pinned NOT-n); storyboards + duration survive (the storyboard lane keeps its fuel); scoped to the WEB-family rungs only (the native-client player answers measured n-free). A doomed native boot never arms again; if YouTube re-softens n (P31's era), re-measure and revert — the comment is the ledger.
- resolveReadTier now also reports userId (additive — the p36 isolation pin re-pointed to the new honest shape).
- PlaybackDto.source gains "player-authed" (the per-user merged answer's disclosure).

Gates: the full 8-suite battery 2181 pass / 0 fail (boot 865 / watch 712 / liveshorts 150 / cutover 111 / auth 133 / community 35 / notifications 41 / p36 134 — +12 net new P41 pins: the dropScrambledNFormats battery ×3 (the dead drop + lookalike survival + same-reference law), the chain's n-filter integration ×1 (n-free wins the rung + all-scrambled degrades to storyboards), the authed-rung battery ×6 (the Bearer request shape + immediate muxed win, the merge dedupe with the authed mint winning, THE PER-USER ISOLATION battery — each user's own token + the shared row never carrying authed fuel + both per-user rows present, the honest walled degrade with no sticky, the 401 refusal sticky skipping the doomed rung, the anonymous no-Authorization-anywhere parity), the route battery ×2 (the connected session's DTO riding the authed rung end-to-end with the stored token + the anonymous byte-identical shape)), tsc clean, lint 0 errors on every touched file.

Stage Summary:
- The operator's connected account is now the answer to the wall's own question: hover previews and the watch-page native fallback draw the user's OWN authenticated formats (n-free ANDROID/ANDROID_VR muxed + IOS audioless preview fuel — the exact shapes measured live) through the same per-user OAuth spine that already powers their likes and subscriptions.
- Anonymous users keep the honest floors (storyboards + the embed lottery) — and never a doomed native boot on n-scrambled fuel.
- THE HONEST RESIDUAL: the authed rung's live behavior on the deployed build (whether Google's innertube endpoint accepts the youtube.force-ssl-scoped token from Vercel's egress, and whether an authenticated datacenter request clears the bot wall) is the one axis the station could not measure without the operator's real token — the fake-bearer 401 proves the header is parsed; the runbook §1 carries the exact operator verification steps (hover a box + open a protected-class watch page signed-in-and-connected). If the token is refused (scope class), the rung degrades honestly to today's behavior and the sticky keeps the cost at zero.

Task ID: P42-NETFLIX-SOURCE
Agent: the resident TL station (direct execution — the operator's second-source directive; the lead-merges law)
Task: the operator's directive (verbatim intent): "add more videos from more platforms and the next platform we pick is Netflix.com … videos from netflix.com should be browsable and playable seemingly natively from the webflix-2.0 interface … without breaking any of its existing webflix-2.0 features."

Work Log:
- RESEARCH (live, 2026-10-24, docs/research/2026-10-24-netflix-integration-options.md): the honest asymmetry measured — Netflix offers no data API, blocks embedding, DRM-protects 100% of streams (Widevine/PlayReady/FairPlay); netflix.com/robots.txt is `User-agent: * / Disallow: /` (respected: zero /title/ or /search page fetches anywhere). EVALUATED AND REJECTED with reasons: Browserbase-style logged-in streaming (ToS: automated access + credential sharing + public performance; detection: the M7353-5101 unblocker wall + datacenter/VM fingerprinting; Widevine L1 quality floor; licensing), DRM stream extraction (DMCA §1201 / EU DSM Art.3 — never), operator-credential automation (account risk + zero upside; the shared password was NEVER stored — rotation advised), third-party catalog APIs (uNoGS/TMDB — no keys provided; P43 lane when one lands).
- THE APPROACH THAT WORKS: Netflix's own public Tudum Top 10 data. Measured live: netflix.com/tudum/top10 server-renders a normalized Relay cache at netflix.reactContext.models.graphql (JSON.parse of a JS-escaped literal) carrying per-title videoId, title, synopsis, releaseYear, maturityRating, artwork (sdpArt/storyArt/logoArt on parameterized urlsSized keys), weeklyRank/weeklyViews/weeklyHoursViewed/cumulativeWeeksInTop10, isPlayable, and per-country ranks for ~93 countries. The 8-URL pool matrix mapped and pinned (weekly/all-time × films/series × EN/non-EN; /tudum/top10, /tv, /films-non-english, /tv-non-english, /most-popular[/tv][/films-non-english][/tv-non-english]).
- ADR 0002 (docs/adr/0002-multi-source-netflix-policy.md): playback = the official deep-link hand-off (netflix.com/watch/<id>, the Apple TV/Google TV pattern — Netflix's own player, zero DRM bytes through WebFlix); reads = the bounded 8-URL matrix through the shared cache; provenance law on every DTO; the source-adapter shape (own upstream seam, own nf:* keyspace, additive Prisma only); the permanent credential ban; non-interference with every youtube surface.
- DATA LAYER (src/lib/netflix/*): upstream.ts (the injectable seam, the youtube mirror), types.ts (NetflixTitleDTO/Rail/Home/TitlePage/Search — provenance `source` on every payload), top10.ts (the pool matrix + the escape-aware literal scanner — exact first-unescaped-quote termination — the Relay-cache parser, the field-wise merge of the Global+geo entity copies, the artwork parameterized-key lookup, 1-retry fetch, cachedResilient with the isEmpty poisoning guard, soft TTL 6h/24h + last-good 48h/7d, the Prisma mirror upsert), catalog.ts (hub composition — 8 rails, Global/US re-ranking from countryRanks, pool→mirror title fallback, the scored catalog search over the mirror, honest degrade shapes), deeplink.ts (the ONLY Netflix URL constructor — ADR §1).
- PRISMA (additive only): NetflixTitle (the mirror/search index; countryRanks as "CC:rank" CSV — SQLite carries no list primitives) + NetflixMyListItem (the WebFlix-LOCAL My Netflix List — Netflix's own list API needs a login ADR §5 forbids) + the User relation.
- ROUTES (force-dynamic, rateLimit-gated, honest 429/400/404/502): GET /api/netflix (the hub), GET /api/netflix/title/[id] (title page; the FULL country-rank truth rides the DTO), GET /api/netflix/search (the mirror search with the honest scope note), GET/POST/DELETE /api/netflix/my-list (resolveViewer identity — the watch-lane demo fallback; anonymous reads honest-empty, writes 401).
- UI: /netflix hub (the Netflix-branded dark surface — hero with backdrop+logo art, ranked rails with the outlined Top-10 numerals, Global/US country chips, the My List rail, the unavailable notice, the source note), /netflix/t/[id] (backdrop hero, metadata row, where-it-ranks chips, the WebFlix-local My List toggle, the in-app trailer through the EXISTING YouTube lane (/api/search + the WebFlix player), same-pool "More like this"), play-handoff.tsx (the ADR made UI: the DRM/licensing explanation, the official deep link as a plain outbound anchor, the destination URL printed, no fake player surface anywhere), netflix-card/rail/hero, the sidebar Netflix row (TvMinimalPlay, main rung after Subscriptions), the additive /search Netflix shelf (renders only when the mirror answers).
- LIVE VERIFICATION (station browser, 2026-10-24): the hub renders the live weekly rail (Animals #1, week 2026-10-18) + the hero + US re-ranking + the My List rail (round-trip through the seeded dev DB); the title page carries the live metadata (Doing Life: TV-MA, 3 weeks, 13.1M hours); the hand-off dialog names Netflix + prints the official URL; /search?q=animals shows the Netflix shelf WITH the YouTube results coexisting (82 result links); the home grid (48 cards), the watch page (the IFrame player + title) — byte-for-byte intact; mobile 375px renders with zero horizontal overflow; netflix.com/watch/81757047 answers the expected logged-out 301 (the user's own browser, logged in, plays directly).
- GATES: the full 9-suite battery 2212 pass / 0 fail (boot 865 / watch 712 / liveshorts 150 / cutover 111 / auth 133 / community 35 / notifications 41 / p36 134 / netflix 31 — +31 new pins: the parser battery on the REAL captured fixture (tests/fixtures/netflix/top10-films.html, 184KB captured bytes) incl. the escape scanner, the kind/all-time gates, the entity merge, the bounded-URL law + the honest 403/garbage degrades; the route battery incl. the my-list roundtrip; the hub/hand-off UI battery), tsc clean, lint 0 errors (the 1 warning is pre-existing in an untouched file). The sidebar-shape pins re-pointed to the extended ladder (the P42 deviation documented in-test).

Stage Summary:
- WebFlix 2.0 is now a TWO-source product: youtube.com (full parity, unchanged — every surface byte-identical, 2181 pre-existing tests green) and netflix.com (the native browsing layer + the official playback hand-off).
- The source-adapter seams (own upstream/DTO/cache keyspace, provenance law, additive Prisma) are the template every future source rides — ADR 0002 is the law.
- THE HONEST RESIDUALS: (1) catalog depth = Netflix's own tracked Top 10 universe (~140 titles, refreshed weekly by Netflix — the honest scope note rides in the search DTO and the hub copy); full-catalog browse/search is the P43 lane when a TMDB/uNoGS key exists; (2) playback is a hand-off BY DESIGN (ADR 0002 §1 — the DRM/licensing reality, not a bug); (3) the rejected paths (Browserbase, DRM extraction, credential automation) are documented with reasons in the research record; the operator's Netflix password was never stored and should be rotated; (4) prod (Neon) needs the two new tables — the push command rides the merge (prisma db push against DATABASE_URL with the Postgres schema).

Task ID: P49-NETFLIX-EVERYWHERE

Task: deepen the Netflix video integration with the two most user-visible Netflix-native behaviors that work TODAY with zero external prerequisites — the Continue Watching personalized rail and Netflix inside the global search suggest. Per docs/work-items/P49-NETFLIX-EVERYWHERE.md.

Work Log:
- CONTINUE WATCHING: additive Prisma NetflixWatchEvent (userId, netflixId, lastPlayedAt, playCount; FK User+NetflixTitle cascade; @@id([userId,netflixId]); index [userId,lastPlayedAt]) + src/lib/netflix/progress.ts (recordWatch = the FK-law mirror upsert first, then an event UPSERT — a replay bumps playCount + refreshes lastPlayedAt, never stacks rows; listContinueWatching newest-first joined; removeWatch) + /api/netflix/progress (GET anon-honest-empty, POST 401/400/404, DELETE the rail's remove affordance; rate-limited force-dynamic). HONESTY LAW: playCount is literally "you pressed play N times" — the streamed DRM player exposes NO position, so NO percent-watched and NO fabricated progress bar (the UI test pins the <progress> ABSENCE). Writes ride fire-and-forget keepalive from the watch lane's kick (every entry/restart; the Live lane records nothing) AND the hand-off dialog's deep-link click (watching on netflix.com is still a play); a failed write never blocks playback.
- THE RAIL: src/components/netflix/continue-watching-rail.tsx — Netflix's own placement (first personalized row, under the billboard, before My List), cards link STRAIGHT into the in-app watch lane ("Resume X in WebFlix"), hover Play circle, the X removes from the row and the row re-reads its truth (the last card leaving takes the rail with it), honest meta ("Last watched … · played N×" — the pure lastPlayedLabel law pinned), anonymous/empty → absent, never a placeholder.
- GLOBAL SUGGEST (ADDITIVE): /api/search/suggest keeps the P3 `suggestions` string[] contract byte-identical and a `netflix` array rides beside it — ≤3 scored mirror matches from the new catalog.suggestNetflixTitles (the catalog-search scoring law + currently-ranking boost, fresh per request, wrapped so Netflix trouble can never delay/break the YT suggest). The dropdown gains the netflix SuggestRow variant (red N badge + "On Netflix", aria "X — on Netflix"); committing navigates STRAIGHT to /netflix/t/<id> via onNetflixCommit (absent → graceful search-page fallback) and NEVER pollutes recents (a title is not a search); the DESTINATION LAW (measured live): badge rows dedupe only against themselves + the staleness guard netflixRowMatches — NEVER against YT texts (YT's own autocomplete for "doing" contains "doing life"; hiding the badge row would hide the DESTINATION — search page vs title page — not a duplicate). Topbar wires onNetflixCommit → the title page.
- THE IDENTITY LAW (the live-measured gap): the first signed-in play 401'd — Netflix's local lanes rode watch-lane resolveViewer only, and a signed-in WebFlix user without a wfx2_uid (never opened a YouTube watch page) resolved anonymous once the dev DB lost its demo row. Fix: src/lib/netflix/viewer.ts netflixViewerId(req) = getSessionUser (the same JWT decode every gated route uses) → ensureShadowUser (the local-comment shadow-user bridge) → the account's own prisma User; else the legacy resolveViewer lanes; else "". Agent: Z.ai Code (webflix-2.0 lane, /home/z/webflix-2.0 — operator: "go")

Work Log:
- Repo re-cloned from origin/main (16f2c2c == P54 — P54 had landed after the prior summary snapshot); deps reinstalled (app + mini-services/netflix-desktop); dev db pushed + seeded; Prisma client regenerated.
- ENVIRONMENT LAW DISCOVERED AND RECORDED: this sandbox's reaper kills every background process between tool calls (setsid/nohup/disown all die; no cron/atd). Persistent dev servers are impossible; all server-dependent verification re-planned as single-call sequences.
- History purge independently re-verified: fresh mirror clone (4,954 objects, 58 refs), 0 refs/pull/* on origin, the harvest actor redacted in its only surviving history version, zero non-redacted credential fallbacks anywhere. Two YouTube-side emails found in captured fixtures (boxstar.in third-party public; yaslencatty the operator's own in a captured SSR fixture + 2 docs) — neither is the purged Netflix literal; recorded as a P56 operator-decision rung.
- PROD BUG 1 (case law): Prisma `contains` is case-insensitive on SQLite but case-sensitive on Postgres; the search lowercased every needle → prod answered catalogSize 79 with titles [] for EVERY query. Fix: SQL text filter removed — the bounded mirror fetched whole (take:1000) and all matching in the JS scorer.
- PROD BUG 2 (stat ceiling): all-time mirror rows carry Netflix's published cumulative hours in the billions (measured: Squid Game S1, 81105887, weeklyHoursViewed 2,205,200,000); the Int column made every read over such a row throw "Conversion failed" — silently eaten by an empty catch (on Postgres the same class throws on write, silently capping the mirror at legacy rows). Fix: weeklyHoursViewed + weeklyViews → BigInt in both schemas; seams convert at the boundary; DTO keeps the number contract; the search catch now logs (F3 law).
- Honest side-trip reverted: the truncated first error misread as a netflixId overflow; a full String-id migration was built and tested, then reverted wholesale once the instrumented catch named the real column — evidence-free churn does not ship.
- Suggest gate: with the SQL pre-filter gone, the scorer's popularity bonus carried non-matches past score>0 (every query answered with the weekly rail); the bonus now ranks within text matches only.
- Vault: rebuilt as 0700/0600 skeleton (names + empty placeholders) + bashrc sourcing + docs/ops/vault-recovery.md (the one-paste re-provisioning runbook). PUSH BLOCKED (no PAT) — commit 6e19608 rides locally, ready.
- Live proof (single-call, real Tudum mirror): hub 8 rails/80 titles; search animals→1, AnImAlS→1, squid→3 (Squid Game S1 first), game→3, lofi→honest 0; the 2.2B-hours row renders on its title page; browser 375px 0 overflow / 0 broken images; both search shelves render.

Stage Summary:
- Gates: tsc clean; eslint 0 errors (1 pre-existing warning); test:netflix 144/0 twice; full 9-suite battery 2332/0 (+3 pins: mixed-case law, structural no-SQL-text-filter law, billions-of-hours round-trip battery).

MVP: Parity Viewer (45 pts)." → both streams end honestly. DB facts: battle final (winnerSide b, finalizedAt stamped); EXACTLY 2 final snapshots (a=7, b=45) + 1 MVP row (side b, viewer, 45). - REAL BUG FOUND BY THE ACCEPTANCE: the first run's DB held THREE copies of the final/MVP snapshots — three concurrent readers (both consoles reloading + the stream tail's new pk floor) raced the naive lazy-finalize update and each wrote the snapshots. THE SETTLE-CLAIM LAW fixes it in pk.ts: the conditional updateMany claims the settle atomically (WHERE status = "active"); only the claimant (count === 1) writes the snapshots, everyone else re-reads the settled row. The battery's own idempotency pin (serial re-reads) never caught it — the concurrency was acceptance-only. pk battery re-green; FULL battery 446/0 twice after the fix; tsc 0.
- GATES: tsc 0; eslint untouched-clean; battery 446/446 ×2; the dev server restarted clean on :3000 after the run.

Work Log:
- BUG A DIAGNOSIS (miniplayer): the persistence architecture (persistent wrapper + portal + the survival matrix) was NEVER the problem for client-side nav — the reset paths were: (1) the ~1/s positionSec mirror was never a restore source (re-creations restored the stale attach-time latch, usually 0); (2) the AppShell bare branch (/signin//signup) rendered WITHOUT the PlayerHostLayer → portal unmount → created?.destroy() → the account roundtrip restarted playback; (3) the blocked-swap (fallback↔iframe) re-created at the stale latch.
- BUG A FIX: `resumeSecOnMount` (youtube-player.tsx) — the layer passes the live mirror; the creation onReady seeks ONCE when no explicit latch exists (≥3s guard, never for live; frozen at mount — the [videoId,startSec] effect lane untouched, no per-tick loadVideoById hazard); the bare branch renders the layer (fixed overlay, zero auth-layout cost). Pins: tests/miniplayer-restore.test.tsx (7: resume/latch-wins/sub-3s/takeover-reset/live-guard + bare-route ×2, MUTABLE-pathname harness).
- BUG B DIAGNOSIS (home): selection was fully deterministic — shared IP-wide caches, fixed slice(0,24), NO seed, NO shuffle, NO per-user identity; not-interested/dont-recommend-channel were written but NEVER read by home.
- BUG B FIX: `getHomeFeed(category, variety?)` — full-pool seeded rotation (the playlist lane's seededShuffle; same seed → same order) + exclusion of the viewer's hidden videos/channels from EVERY rail; the route derives a FRESH seed per request (youtube's measured signed-out reshuffle) and resolves exclusion through the NEW src/lib/watch/home-variety.ts proxy. THE PURGE-SWEEP LAW CAUGHT THE FIRST CUT (direct db import in the read route): refactored to the action-proxy pattern — the route stays db-import-free; the proxy reads ONLY the viewer's own VideoSignal rows + the signaled videos' channel mapping (never inventory for rendering). Category/chips fetch WIDE (48); the compose cursor derives from the unrotated pool (documented trade-off); signed-out → rotation only; best-effort law (any proxy failure → rotation-only, never a feed failure). Pins: 7 in tests/home-compose.test.ts (legacy-order/determinism/order-variety/full-pool-items/exclusion×2/route smoke).
- HONEST TEST RECONCILIATION: the route-level merge-order pin (home-compose) asserted the unrotated merge order THROUGH the route — the route now legitimately rotates per request, so the order law moved to the library level (getHomeFeed, seed null) where it actually lives; the route-level pins keep status/shape/uniqueness.
- GATES: tsc 0; eslint clean on touched files; test:cutover 119/0 (purge-sweep law green via the proxy); test:watch 712/0; the FULL LIVE battery 446/0 ×15 lanes foreground.
- LIVE PROOF: 3 consecutive /api/home calls → 3 distinct recommended orders (rotation working on the real API); browser: home renders 48 watch links (mobile-375 overflow 0px, console clean); the mini region survives the watch → /signin SPA navigation in the real browser (previously destroyed on the bare page).

Stage Summary:

## 2026-11-07 — P101 (the fresh walk of the P100 build: the prod interact battery, G25, the deploy-target trap)

Task ID: P101 (the standing law: every window walks the current build; the walk's findings become the wave)
Date: 2026-11-07
Claim: SHIPPED ecf3187 (LIVE-VERIFIED dpl_Gk9Tuf3cPqq9TJY4FrGWxvgP7UUn, target=production, the standing URL serving it; the ledger's Wave P101 section carries the full battery). The P101 report: docs/research/2026-11-07-p101-watch-party-delete-affordance-g25-report.md.

Work Log:
- THE WALK, IN ORDER: the post-reset audit (the sandbox rebuilt the wrapper; the real repo survived clean at 0bea232 synced with origin; the dev DB seed re-run — `/api/me` 500 → 200) → the gates re-run from scratch (tsc 0; eslint 0/0; TikTok battery 244/0 incl. all six P100 suites) → the prod read lane re-probe (lofigirl `live:true`, "christmas lofi radio 🎄", 297→340 viewers, `via apify`; `b@d` → 400 — the G20 law holds untouched) → the prod OFFLINE walk (`/tiktok/scout2015/live` renders "not live right now" + the checked-just-now copy + outbound "Open on TikTok" + "Back to the channel" — the honest offline verified on prod, not just dev) → the prod SIGNED-IN interact battery the P100 deploy walk had only guest-covered: p101walk@example.com registered through the real `/api/auth/register` (201), signed in through the real `/signin` form, the watch-party send landed as a durable Prisma row (`P101 Walker`, confirmed via the prod chat API), rendered in the panel, and the cursor polls rode `?after=<the row's id>` — the interact write path is now LIVE-VERIFIED signed-in on prod.
- G25 (the walk's catch): the tombstone law was HALF-WIRED. The route (`DELETE /api/tiktok/live/chat/[room]?messageId=`), the lib (`deleteOwnTikTokLiveChatMessage` — idempotent, owner-only, strangers 404 no-existence-leak), the tests (lib + route pins), and the renderer (`m.deleted → "message removed"`) all existed; the UI exposed NO delete control anywhere — a user could send a message they could never remove. Report-first, then closed TDD the same wave.
- THE ID-SPACE TRAP (the walk's second catch, by inspection mid-closure): `ensureShadowUser` derives `wf-<sessionUserId>` while the client session carries the raw User id — the naive own-check `m.sender.id === session.user.id` would have shipped a delete button that NEVER renders on prod. The closure: `shadowUserId` extracted to the client-safe `src/lib/watch/identity.ts` (pure function, no db import — importing from `local-comments.ts` would have dragged Prisma into the client bundle, the import-law class P100's first pass hit), re-exported for every existing importer; client and server now derive the SAME shadow id from the SAME session id; the UI fixtures use the REAL id-space (`wf-u_pip` vs session `u_pip`) so the pins catch this class forever. The optimistic echo carries the shadow id too — a just-sent own message is immediately deletable.
- THE TDD: the three G25 pins landed RED first (3 fail / 11 pass — the tombstone affordance could not exist), then 14/0. The delete-click fixture is STATEFUL (`deleted: deletedChats.length > 0`) — the 4s poll re-answers server truth and must CONFIRM the tombstone, not undo it (the static fixture caught teaching the wrong lesson). A failed delete keeps the row and surfaces the panel's own alert ("The message could not be removed."). Adjacent drift fixed in passing: the lib docstring's "the route maps that to 403" corrected to the route's real 404. Gates: tsc 0; eslint 0/0; FULL battery 3067/0 exit 0 (3064 → 3067).
- THE DEPLOY-TARGET TRAP (recorded for every future ship): the v13 API cut WITHOUT `"target":"production"` produces a PREVIEW deployment — `target: None`, aliased only to `webflix-2-0-git-main-tepa.vercel.app`, while the standing URL keeps serving the old prod build and every API probe still answers identically (G25 changed no API).
